# Add 52 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Add 52 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Date: 2026-10-18 · Review: ready

Snapshot check: upstream heads were not checked; pinned evidence is a historical snapshot.

### Identity (reviewed)

Néstor Sancho’s BIOS-based Z80 assembly game-development libraries for MSX cartridges.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/README.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/config.makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected collision.asm implements player/enemy/bullet tests; cartridge_init.asm configures slot switching, BIOS hooks, VDP, PSG, optional RAM check and turbo R Z80 mode. Genre helpers, cartridge examples, audio/compression dependencies and reference documents form one coherent library. This is original development tooling, not recovered historical game code. Library LICENSE is BSD-3-Clause; external replayers, compressors and copied reference texts retain separate provenance.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/README.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/config.makefile

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Native Z80 instructions establish source architecture; no minimum runtime configuration is inferred.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/README.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/config.makefile

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Inspected native implementation and build establish Z80 code targets, without a verified minimum machine configuration.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/README.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/config.makefile

### Build (reviewed)

config.makefile and examples.makefile invoke tniASM, PCXTOOLS and ZX0/ZX1/Pletter through Windows shell commands. Example ROMs exist, but external tools and compressor submodules must be provided. No upstream source was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/README.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/config.makefile

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

MSX cartridge output is established. Optional CFG_INIT_16KB_RAM test is a configurable library feature, not a universal minimum for every produced cartridge.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/README.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/config.makefile

### AI (no-evidence-found)

Unknown. No explicit development-AI attribution established in the inspected primary files; non-use is not inferred.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/README.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/config.makefile

### Relationships (reviewed)

Canonical library used by already tracked World Rally and Stevedore. A vendored snapshot in either game does not cover this separately developed library.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/README.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-msxlib/blob/e632adb2af85c2c61a52fa3dfa5bb4fff3295f7b/config.makefile

### Identity (reviewed)

Partially annotated disassembly, room-data extractor and graphical/English patches for Casio’s 1986 MSX Youkai Yashiki (Ghost House).
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-youkaiyashiki-patch/blob/94a8a8dab8fa0e51884032559533a1dbe66f78f8/readme.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-youkaiyashiki-patch/blob/94a8a8dab8fa0e51884032559533a1dbe66f78f8/makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

original/YoukaiYashiki.annotated.asm contains labeled Z80 startup, map/room tables and game code. YoukaiYashiki.patched.asm adds relocatable room definitions, new decoding modes and graphical changes; the Java extractor parses room data. Opaque original-ROM incbin ranges remain in the patch: this is not a wholly source-replaced engine. patch/license is CC BY-NC-ND 4.0; author credits @yazioh’s graphics with all rights reserved and Casio’s original game separately.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-youkaiyashiki-patch/blob/94a8a8dab8fa0e51884032559533a1dbe66f78f8/readme.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-youkaiyashiki-patch/blob/94a8a8dab8fa0e51884032559533a1dbe66f78f8/makefile

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Native Z80 instructions establish source architecture; no minimum runtime configuration is inferred.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-youkaiyashiki-patch/blob/94a8a8dab8fa0e51884032559533a1dbe66f78f8/readme.md
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msx-youkaiyashiki-patch/blob/94a8a8dab8fa0e51884032559533a1dbe66f78f8/makefile

### Identity (reviewed)

Java command-line MSX BIOS metadata inspector and experimental field-patching implementation.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/msxbios-tools/blob/94530ae56f32083a2d59437ec3b5ab1c361b729a/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

MsxBiosToolsApp.java implements text and TSV viewers over BIOS files; Msx1HasSlotfix.java checks instruction signatures at $016F and Z80.java supplies opcode-aware checks. Patch.call() modifies an in-memory byte array but never writes outputPath, so advertised persistent patch output is unfinished in this snapshot. Charset binary/PNG examples are reference data, not the project’s sole contribution. No project-wide licence file found in the pinned tree; public availability is not a permissive licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Visual Studio Code extension that statically measures Z80 instruction cycles and byte sizes, with MSX/CPC/Next timing variants.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/z80-asm-meter-vscode/blob/d780538abfb4ca761e72595708b0b4dfcaf4f788/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Z80InstructionParser.ts matches mnemonics/operands against instruction datasets and supports opcode lookup; ExecutionFlowTotalTimingsMeterable.ts adjusts conditional-path timing. Package config includes MSX M1 waits, CPC NOP-based timing and Z80N. Static timing/size analysis is demonstrated, not dynamic emulation. Package and LICENSE specify LGPL-3.0.
- https://github.com/theNestruo/z80-asm-meter-vscode/blob/d780538abfb4ca761e72595708b0b4dfcaf4f788/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

C transcription, enhancements and English translation of the 1984 La piramide di Iunnuh text adventure, with Commodore and TI-99/4A ports.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

innuh.c explicitly says Bucci based the transcription on Manlio Severi’s Spectrum listing, itself derived from Aristide Torrelli’s TI original, and documents old-to-new variable correspondence. Source implements world connectivity, inventory/parser and hazards. This is source-informed transcription, not a binary-derived reconstruction; TI origin alone does not establish analysis of a TI binary. Root LICENSE contains GPL-3.0; historical magazine game attribution remains explicit.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/makefile

### Source CPU (not-applicable)

Transcribed predecessor is a high-level Spectrum listing, not an inspected Z80 binary or assembly; no source CPU is assigned from platform name.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/makefile

### Target CPU (reviewed)

cc65 native Commodore recipes establish 6502-family outputs; inspected TMS9900 startup and explicit tms9900 toolchain establish TI output architecture. Modern terminal host CPU remains unknown.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/makefile

### Build (reviewed)

makefile gives cc65/cl65 Commodore builds, gcc terminal versions and tms9900-as/gcc/ld plus elf2ea5/ea5split TI builds. ti994a_crt0.asm is visible startup source; libti99/libti99.a is a bundled opaque dependency, not fully source-recovered code. No upstream source was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/README.md
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/makefile

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

Author specifies VIC20 +16K, PET 32K and TI99/4A 32K expansion with Editor/Assembler cartridge and disk; TI 80-column mode needs F18A. These are documented author requirements, not independently tested. Modern terminal target host CPU unknown.
- https://github.com/DarwinNE/innuhs_pyramid/blob/c4505f4931035aa59a51937d504c88a1e430481b/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

New UNIX-like microkernel/OS with implemented Human68k .X/.R loaders, F-line syscall translation and native X68000 boot support, alongside several embedded and retro targets.
- https://github.com/toyoshim-i/PiPAPo/blob/5c17333e5999ad4d1171676b22d2ae3d66574d05/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Inspected HU header validation, big-endian relocation processing, native/emulated execution selection and implemented file/console/memory DOS-call bridge. A general compatibility OS, not a game-specific CPU wrapper. SD support and Xtensa/RISC-V paths have documented gaps; do not treat planned full IOCS support as implemented. No root project licence or general source copyright grant found in the bounded tree/documentation review; third_party/fonts has a separate licence. Public source does not establish unrestricted redistribution.

Inspected IWM register/edge-driven controller code and compact-Mac bus implementation. README lists 128K through IIx/SE30/Portable/PowerBook models, 68000/68020/68030 and PMMU/FPU support. MIT licence for Snow; README acknowledges Musashi/MAME as reference and separately attributed components including Fluxfox and BlueSCSI Toolbox.

NE.S labels STinG/MagiCNet tested and MiNTNet untested; do not generalise compatibility. GNU GPL version 2 text and author copyright in source/docs; hardware documentation is also GPL according to README.
- https://github.com/EmmanuelKasper/ethernec/blob/afb8bf40ba3034d00073166e9a0757b72ad589f6/README.TXT

English README explicitly warns its Google Translate rendition can be inaccurate; the language/build implementation is direct evidence. README and wonbe.c state GNU GPL without a version conclusively established in this bounded read.

Preserved period C and 16-bit segmented x86 startup code explicitly target WonderWitch. ### Identity (reviewed)

Small substantive native Game Boy preemptive round-robin scheduler implementation and demonstration. Timer ISR saves task registers/stack pointer, advances a task-control-block ring and restores the next stack; it is more than a scheduling concept or prose tutorial.
- https://github.com/Manucar/ScheduGB/blob/10e2d5b3e97abc0a863d8a89f478709c0d270459/README.md
- https://github.com/Manucar/ScheduGB/blob/10e2d5b3e97abc0a863d8a89f478709c0d270459/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

README calls this a work in progress. Scope is a small reusable runtime component with a demo, not a complete operating system or validated real-time scheduler. No licence file found in the inspected tree, and no explicit general licence established from README.
- https://github.com/Manucar/ScheduGB/blob/10e2d5b3e97abc0a863d8a89f478709c0d270459/README.md
- https://github.com/Manucar/ScheduGB/blob/10e2d5b3e97abc0a863d8a89f478709c0d270459/Makefile

### Source CPU (not-applicable)

Original independently developed homebrew/tool source, without predecessor machine-code recovery. Source CPU is not applicable.

- https://github.com/Manucar/ScheduGB/blob/10e2d5b3e97abc0a863d8a89f478709c0d270459/Makefile

### Target CPU (reviewed)

scheduler.c directly implements SM83-style register-stack operations, ldhl SP offsets and RETI within a GBDK Game Boy target; pinned GBDK documentation identifies that port as SM83.
- https://github.com/Manucar/ScheduGB/blob/10e2d5b3e97abc0a863d8a89f478709c0d270459/README.md
- https://github.com/Manucar/ScheduGB/blob/10e2d5b3e97abc0a863d8a89f478709c0d270459/Makefile

### Build (reviewed)

Makefile invokes GBDK lcc over src C/assembly and links an Example.gb ROM. scheduler.c includes SM83 inline assembly, timer interrupt registration, task stack initialization and first-task launch. The code was read only; context-switch correctness and safe interaction with other interrupts are unverified. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.

### Identity (reviewed)

Independent native DOS implementation of XCOPY-compatible recursive directory copying, date/archive filtering and overwrite handling.
- https://github.com/FDOS/xcopy/blob/940dac05944a8e33c98c97933e80cfda1a96b2a1/README
- https://github.com/FDOS/xcopy/blob/940dac05944a8e33c98c97933e80cfda1a96b2a1/source/xcopy.c

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual recursive xcopy_files and xcopy_file/copy_file behavior inspected, including free-cluster arithmetic and archive attributes. README explicitly lists insufficient-memory exit status as not implemented; full Microsoft equivalence not claimed. README explicitly GNU GPLv2; doc/copying.txt supplied.
- https://github.com/FDOS/xcopy/blob/940dac05944a8e33c98c97933e80cfda1a96b2a1/README
- https://github.com/FDOS/xcopy/blob/940dac05944a8e33c98c97933e80cfda1a96b2a1/source/xcopy.c

### Identity (reviewed)

FreeDOS-Himem-derived XMS manager with alternative HimemX2 allocation strategy and 32-bit extended-memory support.
- https://github.com/Baron-von-Riedesel/HimemX/blob/bbaf6b8951cdac785f1f4e9b67c25439c5bf8e75/Readme.txt
- https://github.com/Baron-von-Riedesel/HimemX/blob/bbaf6b8951cdac785f1f4e9b67c25439c5bf8e75/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

Substantively changed allocator/handle management and A20/unreal-mode memory-move implementation inspected. Original code and runtime are native x86, not a full guest-machine emulator. README explains HimemX2 addresses scattered allocations relevant to ISA DMA. HimemX modifications public domain; inherited FD Himem rights remain GPL and/or Artistic according to README; not a wholly public-domain project.
- https://github.com/Baron-von-Riedesel/HimemX/blob/bbaf6b8951cdac785f1f4e9b67c25439c5bf8e75/Readme.txt

### Identity (reviewed)

Ported and maintained C86 compiler, dev86 assembler/linker/archiver/preprocessor, make and dis88 for native ELKS and modern hosts.
- https://github.com/ghaerr/8086-toolchain/blob/fa8f2a7c44ea5e21b71c3ab7e33899192cb0ffb9/readme.md
- https://github.com/ghaerr/8086-toolchain/blob/fa8f2a7c44ea5e21b71c3ab7e33899192cb0ffb9/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual 8086 expression code generator and assembler source inspected. Distinct native-ELKS adaptation of several historical toolchains, not a duplicate of already-tracked OpenWatcom. dis88 limitations include missing 8087 mnemonics and data-segment symbolic references. Mixed component licences. Crucially compiler/gen86.c reserves commercial rights and permits redistribution only without commercial interest, with full sources and notice. dis88 requires source/notices with distribution. No blanket permissive/GPL claim.
- https://github.com/ghaerr/8086-toolchain/blob/fa8f2a7c44ea5e21b71c3ab7e33899192cb0ffb9/readme.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved/restored Yuzo Koshiro MUCOM88 Z80 MML compiler/driver sources plus modern host music tools using a PC-8801 CPU/OPNA VM.
- https://github.com/onitama/mucom88/blob/2c8adbed5b21a8876bc530925c8308048e254dfc/README.md
- https://github.com/onitama/mucom88/blob/2c8adbed5b21a8876bc530925c8308048e254dfc/README-en.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Primary pc8801src README distinguishes original disk-restored sources from edited version-specific sources; original may differ from latest binary, so byte identity is not claimed. Modern mucomvm.cpp genuinely emulates PC8801FA CPU+OPNA and runs original Z80 code, not a purely native recompilation. Concrete MML compiler/driver preservation and host authoring environment make this a substantive hybrid. CC BY-NC-SA4.0 project source/assets, not an unrestricted open-source licence. fmgen imposes free-distribution/commercial-permission conditions; sample music requires Yuzo Koshiro attribution. Component terms must be preserved.

### Identity (reviewed)

Public third-party OSF/1 archival source tree with Mach/BSD kernel, historical userland/X components and period build machinery.
- https://github.com/calmsacibis995/osf1-10-src/blob/52dcefc059eda40b725df2ede0b0178a2534f648/README.md
- https://github.com/calmsacibis995/osf1-10-src/blob/52dcefc059eda40b725df2ede0b0178a2534f648/usr/opt/OSC200/src/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

README and retained comments say OSF/1 Release1.0, but files carry later DEC1991–1994 additions and ALPHA build targets. Do not present as pristine authenticated 1.0 release. Chain of custody/release authorization unverified; link-only archival record. Inspected DEC headers call material proprietary/confidential and require a valid written licence; i386 startup includes Intel proprietary/NDA notice. OSF/CMU and other component terms vary. Public hosting does not establish redistribution authorization.
- https://github.com/calmsacibis995/osf1-10-src/blob/52dcefc059eda40b725df2ede0b0178a2534f648/README.md

## Research event notes

Broad discovery followed platform catalogues, developer portfolios, source-preservation and tooling links, with programmatic URL screening before detailed inspection. This is a diverse bounded pass, not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Quality and completeness caveats remain explicit; preserved source, native reconstruction, homebrew and modern tooling are distinguished. No candidate code was executed. All earlier catalogue records are preserved.
